###############################################################################
# Slaanesh events
# Namespace: slaanesh (was "spleasure" before the 2026 events refactor)
# Faction: Chaos
#
# The Dark Prince's hold on the vain and the bored, above all the elves:
# how an Asur or Druchii ruler is tempted into the Cult of Pleasure, the gifts
# Slaanesh gives the faithful, how cultists twist outsiders and reshape those
# close to them, the polymorph spell, and the changes of sex Slaanesh grants.
#
# Temptation                   slaanesh.1 - slaanesh.57
# Gifts of the Dark Prince     slaanesh.70 - slaanesh.91
# Twisting                     slaanesh.100 - slaanesh.122
# Quirking                     slaanesh.200 - slaanesh.332
# Polymorph                    slaanesh.400 - slaanesh.425
# Changed by Slaanesh          slaanesh.450 - slaanesh.462
#
# Fired from common/on_actions/wh_on_actions.txt: on_bi_yearly_pulse
# (slaanesh.1), on_birth (slaanesh.462) and on_post_birth (slaanesh.425).
# Decisions in wh_chaos_decisions.txt start the rest: pleasure_twist
# (slaanesh.100), slaanesh_quirk (slaanesh.200) and vassal_polymorph
# (slaanesh.400), each with a chance of a gift (slaanesh.70);
# hide_deformity_spells in wh_magic_decisions.txt fires slaanesh.90. The
# concubine events and Skaven births also call in.
# Scripted effects and triggers: wh_slaanesh_effects.txt,
# wh_slaanesh_triggers.txt
# Artifacts: common/artifacts/26_artifacts_slaanesh.txt
# Loc: localisation/00_slaanesh_events.csv
# Full list with callers: docs/events/event_database.csv
###############################################################################

namespace = slaanesh

###############################################################################
# Temptation
###############################################################################

# slaanesh.1 The whisper of the Dark Prince
# Every two years an Asur or Druchii ruler past forty with a fitting trait can
# be tempted. Picks one temptation the ruler has not already beaten: the
# poet, the scholar, the mystic, the genius, the glutton, the drunkard, the
# lover or the cruel master. Players and seducers are tempted more often.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.1
	hide_window = yes

	only_rulers = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	min_age = 40 # elves only

	trigger = {
		higher_tier_than = BARON
		NOT = { society_member_of = cult_of_pleasure }
		NOT = { has_character_flag = spirituallytempted_slaanesh }
		NOT = { has_character_flag = physicallytempted_slaanesh }
		NOT = { trait = malekith }
		NOT = { trait = everqueen }
		NOT = { has_character_flag = ai_flag_refuse_conversion }
		trait = creature_elf
		NOT = { religion = cult_of_pleasure religion = atharti }
		OR = { religion = cadai religion = cytharai }
		OR = {
			trait = scholar
			trait = poet
			trait = mystic
			trait = brilliant
			trait = quick
			trait = genius
			trait = hedonist
			trait = gluttonous
			trait = drunkard
			trait = cruel
			trait = impaler
			trait = temperate
			trait = chaste
			trait = celibate
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		modifier = { factor = 1.75 ai = no }
		modifier = { factor = 3 ai = no has_focus = focus_seduction }
		modifier = { factor = 0.50 higher_tier_than = DUKE }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = hedonist }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = deceitful }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = gregarious }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = ambitious }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = proud }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = stressed }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = depressed }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = slothful }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = is_fat }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = is_malnourished }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = addict }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = seducer }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = seductress }
		modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = theologian }
		modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = zealous }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = cynical }
		modifier = { factor = 0.9 trait = isolationist_elf }
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.25
			k_avelorn = {
				holder_scope = { trait = everqueen society_member_of = cult_of_pleasure }
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			k_avelorn = { holder_scope = { trait = everqueen religion = cult_of_pleasure } }
		}
		modifier = { factor = 0.75 religion = cytharai religion_authority = 0.50 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.75 religion = cytharai religion_authority = 0.75 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.75 religion = cadai religion_authority = 0.25 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.75 religion = cadai religion_authority = 0.50 }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			#Spiritual call
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = temptation_won_poet }
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = poet } }
				character_event = { id = slaanesh.2 }
			}
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = temptation_won_scholar }
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = scholar } }
				character_event = { id = slaanesh.15 }
			}
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = temptation_won_mystic }
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = mystic } }
				character_event = { id = slaanesh.22 }
			}
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = temptation_won_genius }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					NOT = { trait = bright }
					NOT = { trait = smart }
					NOT = { trait = quick }
					NOT = { trait = brilliant }
					NOT = { trait = genius }
				}
				long_character_event = { id = slaanesh.28 }
			}
			#Physical call
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = temptation_won_gluttonous }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					NOT = { trait = hedonist }
					NOT = { trait = gluttonous }
					NOT = { trait = temperate }
				}
				character_event = { id = slaanesh.33 }
			}
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = temptation_won_drunkard }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					NOT = { trait = hedonist }
					NOT = { trait = drunkard }
					NOT = { trait = temperate }
				}
				character_event = { id = slaanesh.40 }
			}
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = temptation_won_lustful }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					NOT = { trait = lustful }
					NOT = { trait = chaste }
					NOT = { trait = celibate }
				}
				character_event = { id = slaanesh.47 }
			}
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = temptation_won_cruel }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					NOT = { trait = cruel }
					NOT = { trait = kind }
					NOT = { trait = impaler }
				}
				character_event = { id = slaanesh.52 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaanesh.2 The poet's lost muse
# A poet's verses have dried up. Dust and pain both promise inspiration; most
# refuse.
# Scope: ROOT is the poet.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.2
	picture = GFX_evt_library

	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh2 trigger = { religion_group = cadai_group } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh2b trigger = { religion_group = cytharai_group } }
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Use stuff
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh2
		character_event = { id = slaanesh.3 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { # Cut yourself
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh2
		character_event = { id = slaanesh.4 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { # No thanks
		name = EVTOPTCslaanesh2
		set_character_flag = temptation_won_poet
		ai_chance = { factor = 98 }
	}
}

# slaanesh.3 The dust
# The poet breathes the dust: an addict now, with visions to follow
# (slaanesh.5-8).
# Scope: ROOT is the poet.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.3
	picture = GFX_evt_library

	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh3 trigger = { religion_group = cadai_group } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh3b trigger = { religion_group = cytharai_group } }
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh3
		set_character_flag = spirituallytempted_slaanesh
		add_trait = addict
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				40 = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.5 } }
				30 = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.6 } }
				25 = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.7 } }
				5 = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.8 } } # Tripping events
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaanesh.4 The knife
# The poet cuts themselves for inspiration and learns from the pain. A
# songbird comes to the window later (slaanesh.12).
# Scope: ROOT is the poet.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.4
	picture = GFX_evt_library

	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh4 trigger = { religion_group = cadai_group } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh4b trigger = { religion_group = cytharai_group } }
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh4
		set_character_flag = spirituallytempted_slaanesh
		add_trait = wounded
		change_learning = 1
		long_character_event = { id = slaanesh.12 days = 20 random = 60 }
	}
}

# slaanesh.5 Vision: the watchers
# A dust vision that leaves the poet wiser and paranoid. A servant notices
# (slaanesh.9).
# Scope: ROOT is the poet.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.5
	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_demons
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh5

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh5
		change_learning = 1
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = paranoid } } add_trait = paranoid }
		character_event = { id = slaanesh.9 days = 45 random = 50 }
	}
}

# slaanesh.6 Vision: the garden

character_event = {

		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = content } } add_trait = content }
		character_event = { id = slaanesh.9 days = 45 random = 50 }
	}
}

# slaanesh.7 Vision: the statue
# A deep dust vision: much more learning and contentment.
# Scope: ROOT is the poet.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.7
	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_demons
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh7

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh7
		change_learning = 3
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = content } } add_trait = content }
		character_event = { id = slaanesh.9 days = 45 random = 50 }
	}
}

# slaanesh.8 Vision: the open mind
# The rarest dust vision raises the poet's intelligence one step.
# Scope: ROOT is the poet.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.8
	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_demons
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh8

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh8
		if = { limit = { trait = brilliant } remove_trait = brilliant add_trait = genius }
		if = { limit = { trait = quick } remove_trait = quick add_trait = brilliant }
		if = { limit = { trait = smart } remove_trait = smart add_trait = quick }
		if = { limit = { trait = bright } remove_trait = bright add_trait = smart }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { trait = bright }
				NOT = { trait = smart }
				NOT = { trait = quick }
				NOT = { trait = brilliant }
				NOT = { trait = genius }
			}
			add_trait = bright
		}
		character_event = { id = slaanesh.9 days = 45 random = 50 }
	}
}

# slaanesh.9 The servant who saw
# A servant finds the poet's dust. Share it, kill them with it, or give it all
# up.
# Scope: ROOT is the poet.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.9
	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_cult_1
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh9

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Have him join you
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh9
		character_event = { id = slaanesh.10 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 33 }
	}
	option = { # Kill him of overdose
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh9
		character_event = { id = slaanesh.11 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 33 }
	}
	option = { # Stop
		name = EVTOPTCslaanesh9
		clr_character_flag = spirituallytempted_slaanesh
		set_character_flag = temptation_won_poet
		random_list = {
			80 = { }
			20 = { remove_trait = addict }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 33 }
	}
}

	}
}

# slaanesh.15 The scholar's ink
# Alone in the library, a scholar is tempted to rewrite old chronicles
# (deceitful only) or fill them with lewd miniatures.
# Scope: ROOT is the scholar.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.15
	picture = GFX_evt_book_carolingian_script
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh15
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Tweak history (deceitful option)
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh15
		trigger = { trait = deceitful }
		tooltip_info = deceitful
		character_event = { id = slaanesh.16 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { # Put nasty miniatures
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh15
		character_event = { id = slaanesh.17 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { # No thanks
		name = EVTOPTCslaanesh15
		set_character_flag = temptation_won_scholar
		ai_chance = { factor = 98 }
	}
}

	}
}

# slaanesh.18 The curious student
# A student keeps asking about the scholar's work. Send them astray
# (deceitful), seduce them (lustful), push them down the stairs, or stop.
# Scope: ROOT is the scholar.
long_character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.18
	picture = GFX_evt_book_carolingian_script
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh18 trigger = { is_female = yes } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh18b trigger = { is_female = no } }
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Have him lost
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh18
		trigger = { trait = deceitful }
		tooltip_info = deceitful
		character_event = { id = slaanesh.19 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 33 }
	}
	option = { # Give him what he wants
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh18
		trigger = { trait = lustful }
		tooltip_info = lustful
		character_event = { id = slaanesh.20 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 33 }
	}
	option = { # Have him fall from the stairs
		name = EVTOPTCslaanesh18
		character_event = { id = slaanesh.21 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 33 }
	}
	option = { # No thanks
		name = EVTOPTDslaanesh18
		clr_character_flag = spirituallytempted_slaanesh
		set_character_flag = temptation_won_scholar
		ai_chance = { factor = 33 }
	}
}

# slaanesh.22 The mystic's rat
# A mystic's experiment slowly suffocates a lab rat, and the mystic finds it
# fascinating. Watch and repeat, try again, or end it.
# Scope: ROOT is the mystic.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.22
	picture = GFX_evt_lab
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh22
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Observe it and repeat
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh22
		character_event = { id = slaanesh.23 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { # Ignore it and try again
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh22
		character_event = { id = slaanesh.24 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { # Kill it and check the formula
		name = EVTOPTCslaanesh22
		set_character_flag = temptation_won_mystic
		ai_chance = { factor = 98 }
	}
}

# slaanesh.23 Watching it die

# slaanesh.24 Trying again
# The mystic learns from the failure. An inspector arrives (slaanesh.25).
# Scope: ROOT is the mystic.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.24
	picture = GFX_evt_lab
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh24
	is_triggered_only = yes

# slaanesh.33 The feast
# Gluttons hear they eat too much, the temperate are tempted by a dish, and
# hedonists are invited to an orgy of food.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.33
	picture = GFX_evt_feast

	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh33 trigger = { is_female = yes trait = gluttonous } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh33b trigger = { is_female = no trait = gluttonous } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh33c trigger = { is_female = yes trait = temperate } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh33d trigger = { is_female = no trait = temperate } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh33e
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = temperate } NOT = { trait = gluttonous } }
	}
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # They're wrong, I'm fine!
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh33
		trigger = { trait = gluttonous }
		character_event = { id = slaanesh.34 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { # Maybe a little moderation?
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh33
		trigger = { trait = gluttonous }
		random_list = {
			60 = { }
			40 = { remove_trait = gluttonous }
		}
		set_character_flag = temptation_won_gluttonous
		ai_chance = { factor = 98 }
	}
	option = { # I little indulgement won't hurt...
		name = EVTOPTCslaanesh33
		trigger = { trait = temperate }
		character_event = { id = slaanesh.35 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { # No thanks
		name = EVTOPTDslaanesh33
		trigger = { trait = temperate }
		set_character_flag = temptation_won_gluttonous
		ai_chance = { factor = 98 }
	}
	option = { # What's wrong with having some fun?
		name = EVTOPTEslaanesh33
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = temperate } NOT = { trait = gluttonous } }
		random_list = {
			60 = { }
			20 = { add_trait = drunkard }
			20 = { add_trait = gluttonous }
		}
		long_character_event = { id = slaanesh.36 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { # Let's keep it classy
		name = EVTOPTFslaanesh33
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = temperate } NOT = { trait = gluttonous } }
		set_character_flag = temptation_won_gluttonous
		ai_chance = { factor = 98 }
	}
}

# slaanesh.47 The whispered proposal
# A lustful ruler's appetite has run dry and a bard at court offers to help; a
# chaste ruler feels yearnings they have always kept down. Try it, or refuse.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.47
	picture = GFX_evt_whispers

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh47
		trigger = { is_female = yes religion_group = cadai_group trait = lustful }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh47b
		trigger = { is_female = no religion_group = cadai_group trait = lustful }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh47c
		trigger = { is_female = yes religion_group = cytharai_group trait = lustful }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh47d
		trigger = { is_female = no religion_group = cytharai_group trait = lustful }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh47e
		trigger = { is_female = yes religion_group = cadai_group NOT = { trait = lustful } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh47f
		trigger = { is_female = no religion_group = cadai_group NOT = { trait = lustful } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh47g
		trigger = { is_female = yes religion_group = cytharai_group NOT = { trait = lustful } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh47h
		trigger = { is_female = no religion_group = cytharai_group NOT = { trait = lustful } }
	}
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Huuh! Kinky!
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh47
		trigger = { trait = lustful }
		character_event = { id = slaanesh.48 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { # That's not really my sort of things...
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh47
		trigger = { trait = lustful }
		random_list = {
			60 = { }
			40 = { remove_trait = lustful }
		}
		set_character_flag = temptation_won_lustful
		ai_chance = { factor = 99 }
	}
	option = { # Maybe.. just this once?
		name = EVTOPTCslaanesh47
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = lustful } }
		if = { limit = { trait = celibate } remove_trait = celibate }
		random_list = {
			60 = { }
			40 = { add_trait = lustful }
		}
		character_event = { id = slaanesh.50 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { # How dare you!
		name = EVTOPTDslaanesh47
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = lustful } }
		set_character_flag = temptation_won_lustful
		ai_chance = { factor = 99 }
	}
}

# slaanesh.52 The verdict
# A cruel ruler is tripped by a servant (Druchii) or contradicted by a
# courtier over a verdict (Asur) and wants to punish them; a kind ruler is
# pushed too far by a pardoned thief or a mocking court.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.52
	picture = GFX_evt_falconing

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh52
		trigger = { OR = { trait = impaler trait = cruel } religion_group = cytharai_group }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh52b
		trigger = { OR = { trait = impaler trait = cruel } religion_group = cadai_group }
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh52c trigger = { trait = kind religion_group = cytharai_group } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh52d trigger = { trait = kind religion_group = cadai_group } }
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # You miserable wretch!
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh52
		trigger = { OR = { trait = impaler trait = cruel } }
		random_list = {
			70 = { }
			30 = { add_trait = wroth }
		}
		character_event = { id = slaanesh.53 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 2 }
	}
	option = { # You know what? He's beneath me.
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh52
		trigger = { OR = { trait = impaler trait = cruel } }
		random_list = {
			60 = { }
			40 = { remove_trait = cruel }
		}
		set_character_flag = temptation_won_cruel
		ai_chance = { factor = 98 }
	}
	option = { # That's it! Now you're getting it!
		name = EVTOPTCslaanesh52
		trigger = { trait = kind }
		random_list = {
			60 = { }
			40 = { remove_trait = kind }
		}
		character_event = { id = slaanesh.55 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 2 }
	}
	option = { # Don't worry, I can manage...
		name = EVTOPTDslaanesh52
		trigger = { trait = kind }
		set_character_flag = temptation_won_cruel
		ai_chance = { factor = 98 }
	}
}

# slaanesh.53 The punishment
# The cruel ruler takes the lash in hand themselves. Keep going, or stop.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.53
	picture = GFX_evt_falconing
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh53 trigger = { religion_group = cadai_group } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh53b trigger = { religion_group = cytharai_group } }
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # You think it's over? We're just getting started here.
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh53
		set_character_flag = physicallytempted_slaanesh
		character_event = { id = slaanesh.54 }
	}
	option = { # I think you've learned your lession.
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh53
		set_character_flag = temptation_won_cruel
	}
}

}

# slaanesh.57 Into the Cult of Pleasure
# Every temptation ends here: the ruler joins the Cult of Pleasure, gains the
# Twist targeted decision and may keep a Phial of Sweet Dust (1 in 3).
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.57
	picture = GFX_evt_cult_of_pleasure
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh57

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh57
		join_society = cult_of_pleasure
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_artifact = slaanesh_dust_phial } }
			random = { chance = 33 add_artifact = slaanesh_dust_phial }
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Gifts of the Dark Prince
###############################################################################

# slaanesh.70 A gift of the Dark Prince
# After twisting, quirking or polymorphing someone, a cultist can be
# rewarded with a gift: a piercing tongue, horns, beauty, ugliness, madness
# or (rarely) a change of sex (slaanesh.450). Elves are touched less often.
# Fired by the pleasure_twist, slaanesh_quirk and vassal_polymorph decisions
# and by the concubine events.
# Scope: ROOT is the cultist.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.70
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			#Tongue
			#Horns
			#Beauty
			#Ugliness
			#Madness
			#Gender swap
			35 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = creature_elf }
				character_event = { id = slaanesh.71 }
			}
			30 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = creature_elf }
				character_event = { id = slaanesh.72 }
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = creature_elf }
				character_event = { id = slaanesh.73 }
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = creature_elf }
				character_event = { id = slaanesh.74 }
			}
			5 = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.75 } }
			5 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					NOT = { trait = creature_elf }
					NOT = { trait = creature_human }
					NOT = { trait = creature_halfling }
					NOT = { trait = creature_dwarf }
					NOT = { trait = creature_chaos_dwarf }
					NOT = { trait = creature_amazon }
					NOT = { trait = creature_beastman }
				}
				character_event = { id = slaanesh.450 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaanesh.71 The piercing tongue
# The cultist's tongue grows long and barbed. Hide it with magic or intrigue,
# or let it show.
# Scope: ROOT is the cultist.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.71
	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh71

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # hide it with magic
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh71
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_character_modifier = magic_power_1
				has_character_modifier = magic_power_2
				has_character_modifier = magic_power_3
				has_character_modifier = magic_power_4
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 has_character_modifier = magic_power_1 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 has_character_modifier = magic_power_2 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_character_modifier = magic_power_3 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.75 has_character_modifier = magic_power_4 }
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 has_character_flag = hidden_mutation }
				character_event = { id = slaanesh.76 days = 15 random = 23 }
			} #Success
			50 = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.77 days = 15 random = 24 } } #Failure
		}
	}
	option = { # hide it with intrigue
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh71
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		random_list = {
			40 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 NOT = { intrigue = 10 } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 intrigue = 10 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 intrigue = 15 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.75 intrigue = 20 }
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 has_character_flag = hidden_mutation }
				character_event = { id = slaanesh.76 days = 15 random = 23 }
			} #Success
			60 = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.77 days = 15 random = 24 } } #Failure
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaanesh71
		add_trait = piercing_tongue
		change_variable = { which = "mutation_counter" value = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { slaanesh_open_worshipper_trigger = no }
			increase_visibility_effect = yes # increase visibility
		}
	}
}

# slaanesh.72 The horns
# The cultist grows horns. Hide them with magic or intrigue, or let them show;
# a hidden cultist who shows them is exposed (slaanesh.79).
# Scope: ROOT is the cultist.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.72
	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh72

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # hide it with magic
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh72
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_character_modifier = magic_power_1
				has_character_modifier = magic_power_2
				has_character_modifier = magic_power_3
				has_character_modifier = magic_power_4
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 has_character_modifier = magic_power_1 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 has_character_modifier = magic_power_2 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_character_modifier = magic_power_3 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.75 has_character_modifier = magic_power_4 }
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 has_character_flag = hidden_mutation }
				character_event = { id = slaanesh.76 days = 15 random = 23 }
			} #Success
			50 = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.78 days = 15 random = 24 } } #Failure
		}
	}
	option = { # hide it with intrigue
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh72
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		random_list = {
			40 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 NOT = { intrigue = 10 } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 intrigue = 10 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 intrigue = 15 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.75 intrigue = 20 }
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 has_character_flag = hidden_mutation }
				character_event = { id = slaanesh.76 days = 15 random = 23 }
			} #Success
			60 = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.78 days = 15 random = 24 } } #Failure
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaanesh72
		add_trait = horns_1
		change_variable = { which = "mutation_counter" value = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { slaanesh_open_worshipper_trigger = no }
			character_event = { id = slaanesh.79 } #cultist exposed only when hidden
			increase_visibility_effect = yes # increase visibility
		}
	}
}

# slaanesh.73 Beauty
# The cultist's looks improve one step.
# Scope: ROOT is the cultist.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.73
	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_cult_1
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh73

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh73
		change_variable = { which = "mutation_counter" value = 1 }
		if = { limit = { trait = pretty } remove_trait = pretty add_trait = beautiful }
		else_if = { limit = { trait = fair } remove_trait = fair add_trait = pretty }
		else_if = {
			limit = { OR = { trait = plain trait = ugly trait = unsightly } }
			hidden_effect = { remove_trait = plain remove_trait = ugly remove_trait = unsightly }
			add_trait = fair
		}
		else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = beautiful } } add_trait = fair }
	}
}

# slaanesh.74 Ugliness
# The cultist's looks worsen one step.
# Scope: ROOT is the cultist.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.74
	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_cult_2
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh74

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh74
		change_variable = { which = "mutation_counter" value = 1 }
		if = { limit = { trait = ugly } remove_trait = ugly add_trait = unsightly }
		else_if = { limit = { trait = plain } remove_trait = plain add_trait = ugly }
		else_if = {
			limit = { OR = { trait = pretty trait = beautiful trait = fair } }
			hidden_effect = { remove_trait = pretty remove_trait = fair remove_trait = beautiful }
			add_trait = plain
		}
		else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = unsightly } } add_trait = plain }
	}
}

# slaanesh.75 Madness

character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.75
	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_cultist

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh75
		hidden_effect = {
			change_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 1 }
			character_event = { id = mutate.5 }
		}
	}
}

# slaanesh.79 Exposed
# The horned cultist stops hiding and takes up the open Slaaneshi faith of
# their people. Their courtiers, vassals and liege all hear of it
# (slaanesh.80, 81).
# Scope: ROOT is the cultist.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.79
	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh79

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh79
		slaanesh_open_worship_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			any_courtier = {
				limit = { is_adult = yes }
				character_event = { id = slaanesh.80 days = 1 }
			}
			any_vassal = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.80 days = 1 } }
			if = {
				limit = { independent = no }
				liege = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.81 days = 1 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaanesh.80 A cultist revealed (court and vassals)
# A courtier or vassal hears the news. Fellow cultists may come out too;
# everyone else who is not a Slaaneshi brands the cultist a heretic.
# Scope: ROOT is the courtier or vassal, FROM the exposed cultist.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.80
	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh80

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Time to come out
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh80
		trigger = { society_member_of = cult_of_pleasure }
		slaanesh_open_worship_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
	}
	option = { # Oh dear, how unexpected!
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh80
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { society_member_of = cult_of_pleasure }
				slaanesh_open_worshipper_trigger = no
			}
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_confirmed_heretic who = FROM years = 1000 }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 70 }
	}
}

# slaanesh.81 A cultist revealed (liege)
# As slaanesh.80, for the exposed cultist's liege.
# Scope: ROOT is the liege, FROM the exposed cultist.
character_event = {

	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh81

				NOT = { society_member_of = cult_of_pleasure }
				slaanesh_open_worshipper_trigger = no
			}
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_confirmed_heretic who = FROM years = 1000 }

	}
}

# slaanesh.90 Hiding the flesh
# The hide deformity spell takes away one mutation, the fattest or thinnest
# figure, or a disfigurement for a while. Clever casters with strong magic do
# best; a failure backfires (slaanesh.91).
# Fired by the hide_deformity_spells decision.
# Scope: ROOT is the caster.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.90
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh90
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				trait = mut_spines
				trait = mut_horns
				trait = mut_scales
				trait = mut_thirdeye
				trait = mut_tongue
				trait = mut_crests
				trait = mut_doublehead
				trait = mut_beak
				trait = mut_teeth
				trait = mut_eyes
				trait = mut_bigeyes
			}
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh90b
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				trait = mut_spines
				trait = mut_horns
				trait = mut_scales
				trait = mut_thirdeye
				trait = mut_tongue
				trait = mut_crests
				trait = mut_doublehead
				trait = mut_beak
				trait = mut_teeth
				trait = mut_eyes
				trait = mut_bigeyes
			}
			OR = { trait = disfigured trait = scarred trait = scarred_mid trait = scarred_high }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh90c
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				trait = mut_spines
				trait = mut_horns
				trait = mut_scales
				trait = mut_thirdeye
				trait = mut_tongue
				trait = mut_crests
				trait = mut_doublehead
				trait = mut_beak
				trait = mut_teeth
				trait = mut_eyes
				trait = mut_bigeyes
				trait = disfigured
				trait = scarred
			}
			trait = is_fat
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh90d
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				trait = mut_spines
				trait = mut_horns
				trait = mut_scales
				trait = mut_thirdeye
				trait = mut_tongue
				trait = mut_crests
				trait = mut_doublehead
				trait = mut_beak
				trait = mut_teeth
				trait = mut_eyes
				trait = mut_bigeyes
				trait = disfigured
				trait = scarred
			}

			trait = is_malnourished
		}
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_mage_lore_slaanesh

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh90
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					trait = mut_doublehead
					trait = mut_beak
					trait = mut_bigeyes
					trait = mut_horns
					trait = mut_thirdeye
					trait = mut_spines
					trait = mut_scales
					trait = mut_tongue
					trait = mut_crests
					trait = mut_teeth
					trait = mut_eyes
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = hidden_mut_slaanesh }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = hidden_undernourished }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = hidden_fat }
			}
			if = { limit = { trait = mut_doublehead } remove_trait = mut_doublehead }
			else_if = { limit = { trait = mut_bigeyes } remove_trait = mut_bigeyes }
			else_if = { limit = { trait = mut_beak } remove_trait = mut_beak }
			else_if = { limit = { trait = mut_horns } remove_trait = mut_horns }
			else_if = { limit = { trait = mut_thirdeye } remove_trait = mut_thirdeye }
			else_if = { limit = { trait = mut_spines } remove_trait = mut_spines }
			else_if = { limit = { trait = mut_scales } remove_trait = mut_scales }
			else_if = { limit = { trait = mut_tongue } remove_trait = mut_tongue }
			else_if = { limit = { trait = mut_crests } remove_trait = mut_crests }
			else_if = { limit = { trait = mut_teeth } remove_trait = mut_teeth }
			else_if = { limit = { trait = mut_eyes } remove_trait = mut_eyes }
			add_character_modifier = { name = hidden_mut_slaanesh duration = 300 hidden = yes }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				trait = is_malnourished
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = hidden_disfigurement }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = hidden_undernourished }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = hidden_fat }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = hidden_mut_slaanesh }
			}
			remove_trait = is_malnourished
			add_character_modifier = { name = hidden_undernourished duration = 300 hidden = yes }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				trait = is_fat
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = hidden_disfigurement }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = hidden_undernourished }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = hidden_fat }
			}
			remove_trait = is_fat
			add_character_modifier = { name = hidden_fat duration = 300 hidden = yes }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = { trait = disfigured trait = scarred trait = scarred_mid trait = scarred_high }
			}
			if = { limit = { trait = disfigured } remove_trait = disfigured }
			else_if = {
				limit = { trait = scarred_high }
				remove_trait = scarred_high
				add_trait = scarred_mid
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { trait = scarred_mid }
				remove_trait = scarred_mid
				add_trait = scarred
			}
			else_if = { limit = { trait = scarred } remove_trait = scarred }
			add_character_modifier = { name = hidden_disfigurement duration = 300 hidden = yes }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				60 = {
					modifier = { factor = 1.25 has_character_modifier = magic_power_2 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_character_modifier = magic_power_3 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.75 has_character_modifier = magic_power_4 }
					modifier = { factor = 2.25 trait = genius }
					modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = brilliant }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = quick }
					modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = smart }
					modifier = { factor = 1.1 trait = bright }
					modifier = { factor = 1.1 learning = 12 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.1 learning = 14 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.1 learning = 16 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.1 learning = 18 }
				}
				40 = { #Backfire
					modifier = { factor = 0 is_doom_very_low_trigger = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 2 NOT = { learning = 12 } }
					modifier = { factor = 2 is_doom_low_trigger = yes }
					character_event = { id = slaanesh.91 days = 150 random = 133 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaanesh.91 The spell backfires
# What was hidden comes back worse, and the caster loses piety and prestige.
# Scope: ROOT is the caster.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.91
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh91 trigger = { has_character_modifier = hidden_fat } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh91b
		trigger = { has_character_modifier = hidden_undernourished }
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh91c trigger = { has_character_modifier = hidden_disfigurement } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh91d trigger = { has_character_modifier = hidden_mut_slaanesh } }

	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_cultist

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh91
		piety = -100
		prestige = -100
		hidden_effect = {
			if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = hidden_fat } add_trait = is_fat }
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = hidden_undernourished }
				add_trait = is_malnourished
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = hidden_disfigurement }
				add_trait = disfigured
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = hidden_mut_slaanesh }
				random_list = {
					10 = { add_trait = mut_doublehead }
					10 = { add_trait = mut_beak }
					10 = { add_trait = mut_bigeyes }
					10 = { add_trait = mut_horns }
					10 = { add_trait = mut_thirdeye }
					10 = { add_trait = mut_tongue }
					10 = { add_trait = mut_spines }
					10 = { add_trait = mut_scales }
					10 = { add_trait = mut_teeth }
					10 = { add_trait = mut_crests }
					10 = { add_trait = mut_eyes }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Twisting
###############################################################################

# slaanesh.100 A twist begins
# A cultist has chosen to twist someone (pleasure_twist). The count of
# rounds starts at zero and the cultist picks a way in (slaanesh.102).
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROMFROM the cultist.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.100
	hide_window = yes
	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		set_variable = { which = slaanesh_twist_rounds value = 0 }
		FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.102 days = 10 random = 15 } }
	}
}

# slaanesh.101 Another round
# The cultist keeps tempting: back to the choice of approach (slaanesh.102).
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROM the cultist.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.101
	hide_window = yes
	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.102 days = 10 random = 15 } }
	}
}

# slaanesh.102 How to twist them
# The cultist chooses: toy with the target's mind (paranoia or pride), shame
# them into envy, tease their cravings, or inflame their lust. Spiritual and
# physical temptations open different ways.
# Scope: ROOT is the cultist, FROM the target.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.102
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh102
	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_cultist

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Toy with his mind
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh102
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = spirituallytempted_slaanesh
				society_member_of = cult_of_pleasure
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.112 days = 10 random = 15 } } }
				50 = { FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.109 days = 10 random = 15 } } }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Shame him
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh102
		trigger = { has_character_flag = spirituallytempted_slaanesh }
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.103 } } }
	}

	option = { # Tease his cravings
		name = EVTOPTCslaanesh102
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = physicallytempted_slaanesh
				society_member_of = cult_of_pleasure
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				80 = { FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.115 days = 10 random = 15 } } }
				20 = { FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.118 days = 10 random = 15 } } }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Enflame his lust
		name = EVTOPTDslaanesh102
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = physicallytempted_slaanesh
				society_member_of = cult_of_pleasure
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.120 days = 10 random = 15 } } }
	}

	option = { # Changed my mind
		name = EVTOPTEslaanesh102
		remove_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_twisting }
		FROM = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_recently_twisted years = 1 } }
	}
}

# slaanesh.103 Envy
# The target hears of a rival's success: envious (the cultist's way in) or
# glad for them, which may make them kind.
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROM the cultist.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.103
	picture = "GFX_evt_shadowy_cabal"
	hide_from = yes
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh103 trigger = { is_female = yes } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh103b trigger = { is_female = no } }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh103
		trigger = { is_female = yes }
		add_trait = envious
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.104 } } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh103
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.105 } } }
		trigger = { is_female = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = gregarious NOT = { trait = wroth } }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = kind
				hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = 38268 } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = wroth NOT = { trait = gregarious } }
			random = {
				chance = 10

				add_trait = kind
				hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = 38268 } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = wroth } NOT = { trait = gregarious } }
			random = {
				chance = 22

				add_trait = kind
				hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = 38268 } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = wroth trait = gregarious }
			random = {
				chance = 22

				add_trait = kind
				hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = 38268 } }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaanesh103
		trigger = { is_female = no }
		add_trait = envious
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.104 } } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDslaanesh103
		trigger = { is_female = no }
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.105 } } }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = gregarious NOT = { trait = wroth } }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = kind
				hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = 38268 } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = wroth NOT = { trait = gregarious } }
			random = {
				chance = 10

				add_trait = kind
				hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = 38268 } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = wroth } NOT = { trait = gregarious } }
			random = {
				chance = 22

				add_trait = kind
				hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = 38268 } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = wroth trait = gregarious }
			random = {
				chance = 22

				add_trait = kind
				hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = 38268 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaanesh.104 Envy takes root
# The target is envious. Keep tempting (at most three rounds) or invite them
# in (slaanesh.106).
# Scope: ROOT is the cultist, FROM the target.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.104
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh104
	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_cultist

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Keep tempting
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh104
		trigger = {
			FROM = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = slaanesh_twist_rounds value = 3 } } }
		}
		FROM = { change_variable = { which = slaanesh_twist_rounds value = 1 } }
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.101 } } }
	}
	option = { # Approach
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh104
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.106 } } }
	}
}

# slaanesh.106 The invitation
# The cultist invites the target to the Cult of Pleasure. Accepting makes the
# two cultist friends; refusing sours the target on the cultist for a year.
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROM the cultist.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.106
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh106
	picture = GFX_evt_cult_of_pleasure
	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Accept invitation
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh106
		join_society = cult_of_pleasure
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { set_character_flag = spirituallytempted_slaanesh }
				50 = { set_character_flag = physicallytempted_slaanesh }
			}
			FROM = {
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_cultist_friend years = 1000 }
				character_event = { id = slaanesh.107 }
			}
		}
		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_cultist_friend years = 1000 }

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20
			modifier = { factor = 0 trait = malekith }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = lustful }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = gluttonous }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = drunkard }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = addict }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = hedonist }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = slothful }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = content }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = greedy }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = envious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 2.5 society_member_of = cult_of_pleasure }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = celibate }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = chaste }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = diligent }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = temperate }
			modifier = { factor = 0.95 trait = humble }
			modifier = { factor = 0.95 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 0.01 trait = everqueen }
			modifier = { factor = 0.95 trait = phoenix_king }
		}
	}
	option = { # Refuse invitation
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh106
		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_recently_twisted years = 1 }
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.108 } } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 120
			modifier = { factor = 0 society_member_of = cult_of_pleasure }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = poet }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = mystic }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = seducer }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = seductress }
		}
	}
}

# slaanesh.107 Accepted

	picture = GFX_evt_cult_of_pleasure

		prestige = 50
		remove_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_twisting }

	}
}

	}
}

				hidden_effect = {

						id = 38250 #Notify Trusting
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = gregarious } NOT = { trait = deceitful } }
			random = {
				chance = 22

				add_trait = trusting
				hidden_effect = {
					character_event = {
						id = 38250 #Notify Trusting
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = gregarious trait = deceitful }
			random = {
				chance = 22

				add_trait = trusting
				hidden_effect = {
					character_event = {
						id = 38250 #Notify Trusting
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaanesh.112 Pride

	id = slaanesh.112
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh112

		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh112
		add_trait = proud
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.113 } } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh112
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.114 } } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = { trait = ugly trait = plain trait = unsightly }
				NOT = { trait = ambitious }
			}
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = humble
				hidden_effect = {
					character_event = {
						id = 38260 #Notify Humble
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = ambitious NOT = { trait = ugly trait = plain trait = unsightly } }
			random = {
				chance = 10

				add_trait = humble
				hidden_effect = {
					character_event = {
						id = 38260 #Notify Humble
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { trait = ugly trait = plain trait = unsightly }
				NOT = { trait = ambitious }
			}
			random = {
				chance = 22

				add_trait = humble
				hidden_effect = {
					character_event = {
						id = 38260 #Notify Humble
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { trait = ugly trait = plain trait = unsightly } trait = ambitious }
			random = {
				chance = 22

				add_trait = humble
				hidden_effect = {
					character_event = {
						id = 38260 #Notify Humble
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaanesh.115 The banquet
# A rich banquet: the target turns gluttonous (the cultist's way in) or
# temperate.

character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.115
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh115
	picture = "GFX_evt_feast"
	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh115
		add_trait = gluttonous
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.116 } } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh115
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.117 } } }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = zealous NOT = { trait = greedy } }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = temperate
				hidden_effect = {
					character_event = {
						id = 38276 #Notify Temperate
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = greedy NOT = { trait = zealous } }
			random = {
				chance = 10

				add_trait = temperate
				hidden_effect = {
					character_event = {
						id = 38276 #Notify Temperate
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = greedy } NOT = { trait = zealous } }
			random = {
				chance = 22

				add_trait = temperate
				hidden_effect = {
					character_event = {
						id = 38276 #Notify Temperate
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = greedy trait = zealous }
			random = {
				chance = 22

				add_trait = temperate
				hidden_effect = {
					character_event = {
						id = 38276 #Notify Temperate
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaanesh.116 Gluttony takes root
# The target is gluttonous. Keep tempting or invite them in.
# Scope: ROOT is the cultist, FROM the target.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.116
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh116
	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_cultist

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Keep tempting
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh116
		trigger = {
			FROM = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = slaanesh_twist_rounds value = 3 } } }
		}
		FROM = { change_variable = { which = slaanesh_twist_rounds value = 1 } }
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.101 } } }
	}
	option = { # Approach
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh116
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.106 } } }
	}
}

# slaanesh.118 A fine meal
# A fine meal and finer wine: the target takes to drink, or eats until sick.
# Either way the cultist is pleased.

	id = slaanesh.118
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh118
	picture = GFX_evt_drunk
	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh118
		add_trait = drunkard
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.119 } } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh118
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.116 } } }
		if = { limit = { trait = gluttonous } add_food_poisoning_symptom_effect = yes }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = gluttonous } } add_trait = gluttonous }
	}
}

# slaanesh.120 Lust
# A tryst is offered: the target turns lustful (the cultist's way in) or
# chaste.
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROM the cultist.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.120
	picture = "GFX_evt_lovers"
	hide_from = yes

	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh120 trigger = { is_female = yes NOT = { trait = homosexual } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh120b trigger = { is_female = yes trait = homosexual } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh120c trigger = { is_female = no NOT = { trait = homosexual } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh120d trigger = { is_female = no trait = homosexual } }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh120
		add_trait = lustful
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.121 } } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh120
		hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.122 } } }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = arbitrary NOT = { trait = proud } }
			random = {
				chance = 45

				add_trait = chaste
				hidden_effect = {
					character_event = {
						id = 38274 #Notify Chaste
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = proud NOT = { trait = arbitrary } }
			random = {
				chance = 10

				add_trait = chaste
				hidden_effect = {
					character_event = {
						id = 38274 #Notify Chaste
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = proud } NOT = { trait = arbitrary } }
			random = {
				chance = 22

				add_trait = chaste
				hidden_effect = {
					character_event = {
						id = 38274 #Notify Chaste
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = proud trait = arbitrary }
			random = {
				chance = 22

				add_trait = chaste
				hidden_effect = {
					character_event = {
						id = 38274 #Notify Chaste
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Quirking
###############################################################################

# slaanesh.200 A quirk begins
# A cultist of rank or a Slaaneshi has chosen to change someone close to them
# (slaanesh_quirk). The cultist decides what to change (slaanesh.201).
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROMFROM the cultist.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.200
	hide_window = yes
	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.201 days = 1 } }
	}
}

# slaanesh.201 What to change
# The cultist dislikes how the target looks or how they act, or changes
# their mind.
# Scope: ROOT is the cultist, FROM the target.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.201
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh201
	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_cultist

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Don't like how he/she looks
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh201
		set_character_flag = s_changing_looks
		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = slaanesh.202 days = 1 }
			character_event = { id = slaanesh.203 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				FROM = {
					trait = disfigured
					OR = { trait = is_fat trait = is_malnourished }
					OR = {
						trait = mut_horns
						trait = mut_scales
						trait = mut_crests
						trait = mut_tongue
						trait = mut_thirdeye
						trait = mut_spines
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = dim }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = slow }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = imbecile }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = idiot }
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				FROM = {
					NOT = { trait = beautiful }
					NOT = { trait = unsightly }
					NOT = { trait = is_fat }
					NOT = { trait = is_malnourished }
					NOT = { trait = disfigured }
				}
			}
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 spouse = { character = FROM } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 any_child = { character = FROM } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.25
				OR = {
					AND = { is_female = yes FROM = { NOT = { is_female = yes } } }
					AND = { NOT = { is_female = yes } FROM = { is_female = yes } }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.75
				OR = {
					AND = {
						FROM = { NOT = { age = 50 } }
						FROM = { OR = { trait = creature_human trait = creature_amazon } }
					}
					AND = { FROM = { NOT = { age = 1200 } } FROM = { trait = creature_elf } }
					AND = {
						FROM = { NOT = { age = 200 } }
						FROM = { OR = { trait = creature_dwarf trait = creature_chaos_dwarf } }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Don't like how he/she acts
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh201
		set_character_flag = s_changing_behavior
		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = slaanesh.202 days = 1 }
			character_event = { id = slaanesh.203 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = deceitful }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = bright }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = smart }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = brilliant }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = genius }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = quick }
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				FROM = {
					NOT = { trait = cruel }
					NOT = { trait = lustful }
					NOT = { trait = envious }
					NOT = { trait = drunkard }
					NOT = { trait = hedonist }
					NOT = { trait = depressed }
				}
			}
			modifier = { factor = 0.85 spouse = { character = FROM } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 any_child = { character = FROM } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 NOT = { dynasty = FROM } }
		}
	}
	option = { # Changed my mind
		name = EVTOPTCslaanesh201
		remove_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_quirking }
		FROM = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_recently_quirked years = 1 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# slaanesh.202 Into the quirk
# Passes the choice on to the cultist (slaanesh.204).
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROM the cultist.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.202
	hide_window = yes
	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.204 } }
	}
}

# slaanesh.203 Integrity
# Works out how hard the target is to change: race, learning, virtues and
# faith add to it; vices, madness and Slaaneshi faith take away. The purest
# reach about 45.
# Scope: ROOT is the target.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.203
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { trait = creature_human trait = creature_amazon } }
			set_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 2 }
		}
		if = { limit = { trait = creature_elf } set_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 3 } }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = creature_dwarf }
			set_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 4 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = creature_chaos_dwarf }
			set_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 }
		}
		if = { limit = { trait = everqueen } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 9 } }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = phoenix_king }
			change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 7 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = avatar_kurnous }
			change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 15 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = avatar_isha }
			change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 15 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { religion_group = old_world_group }
			change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 3 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					trait = vampire_lahmian_visible
					trait = vampire_carstein_visible
					trait = vampire_blood_visible
					trait = vampire_strigoi_visible
					trait = vampire_necrarch_visible
					trait = vampire_jade_visible
					trait = vampire_mahtmasi_visible
					trait = vampire_lahmian_hidden
					trait = vampire_carstein_hidden
					trait = vampire_blood_hidden
					trait = vampire_strigoi_hidden
					trait = vampire_necrarch_hidden
					trait = vampire_jade_hidden
					trait = vampire_mahtmasi_hidden
				}
			}
			change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 }
		}
		if = { limit = { learning = 5 } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { learning = 8 } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { learning = 10 } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { learning = 12 } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 2 } }
		if = { limit = { learning = 15 } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 2 } }
		if = { limit = { learning = 20 } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 2 } }
		if = { limit = { trait = scholar } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = theologian }
			change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = administrator }
			change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = scholarly_theologian }
			change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = mastermind_theologian }
			change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 2 }
		}
		if = { limit = { trait = celibate } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 5 } }
		if = { limit = { trait = chaste } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 2 } }
		if = { limit = { trait = temperate } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 3 } }
		if = { limit = { trait = diligent } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { trait = humble } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { trait = bright } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { trait = smart } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { trait = quick } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { trait = brilliant } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { trait = genius } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 2 } }
		if = { limit = { trait = honest } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { trait = brave } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { trait = just } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 2 } }
		if = { limit = { trait = ambitious } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { trait = zealous } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 2 } }
		if = { limit = { trait = paranoid } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { trait = eunuch } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 4 } }
		if = {

			change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 3 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { trait = possessed }
			change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -4 }
		}
		if = { limit = { trait = stressed } change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -1 } }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = depressed }
			change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { society_member_of = cult_of_slaanesh }
			change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -3 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { society_member_of = cult_of_slaanesh2 }
			change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -4 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { society_member_of = cult_of_slaanesh3 }
			change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -5 }
		}
	}
}

# slaanesh.204 Reading the target
# The cultist sizes up the target's integrity and picks an approach: flattery,
# shame or (for their liege or a daemon) a reprimand for looks; temptation,
# trickery or trauma for behaviour.
# Scope: ROOT is the cultist, FROM the target.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.204
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh204
		trigger = { NOT = { FROM = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } } } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh204b
		trigger = {
			FROM = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
			NOT = { FROM = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 10 } } }

				FROM = { OR = { trait = dim trait = slow trait = idiot trait = imbecile } }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = proud } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = gregarious } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = hedonist } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.25
				FROM = { OR = { trait = fair trait = beautiful trait = pretty } }
			}

	}
	option = {

# slaanesh.205 Flattered
# The target is flattered about their looks. Taking it wears integrity down;
# refusing builds it up.
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROM the cultist.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.205
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh205
	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_tempt

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Acknowledge flattery
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh205
		random_list = {
			50 = { change_diplomacy = 1 set_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
			50 = { }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = fake_skill_s } #Slaanesh rigs the game for your enjoyment
				random_list = {
					99 = { change_diplomacy = -1 clr_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
					1 = { clr_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -5 } }
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -3 } }
			}
		}
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.211 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 40
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = deceitful } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = bright } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = smart } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = brilliant } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = genius } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = quick } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = dim }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = slow }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = idiot }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = imbecile }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = hedonist }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 OR = { trait = fair trait = beautiful trait = pretty } }
		}
	}
	option = { # Refuse flattery
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh205
		set_character_flag = refused_flattery_start
		prestige = -25
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 5 } }
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 3 } }
			}
		}
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.211 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 60
			modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = honest } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = wroth } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = cruel } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = slothful } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 FROM = { trait = lunatic_3 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = lunatic_2 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = lunatic_1 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = just } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = quick }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = smart }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = brilliant }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = genius }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = shy }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = humble }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 OR = { trait = plain trait = ugly trait = unsightly } }
		}
	}
}

# slaanesh.209 Tricked
# The target is tricked into behaving differently.
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROM the cultist.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.209
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh209
	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_cultist

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Tricked
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh209
		random_list = {
			50 = { change_intrigue = 1 set_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
			50 = { }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = fake_skill_s } #Slaanesh rigs the game for your enjoyment
				random_list = {
					99 = { change_intrigue = -1 clr_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
					1 = { clr_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -5 } }
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -3 } }
			}
		}
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.215 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 40
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = patient } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = deceitful } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = bright } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = smart } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = brilliant } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = genius } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = quick } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = dim }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = slow }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = idiot }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = imbecile }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = trusting }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = shy }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = kind }
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.25
				FROM = { OR = { trait = fair trait = pretty trait = beautiful } }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Discovered trick
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh209
		set_character_flag = refused_trick_start
		prestige = -25
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 5 } }
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 3 } }
			}
		}
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.215 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 60
			modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = honest } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = wroth } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = trusting } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = slothful } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 FROM = { trait = lunatic_3 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = lunatic_2 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = lunatic_1 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = arbitrary } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = bright }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = quick }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = brilliant }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = genius }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = paranoid }
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.25
				FROM = { OR = { trait = plain trait = ugly trait = unsightly } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaanesh.210 Traumatized
# The target is frightened into behaving differently.
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROM the cultist.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.210
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh210
	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_dungeon

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Traumatized
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh210
		random_list = {
			50 = { change_martial = 1 set_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
			50 = { }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = fake_skill_s } #Slaanesh rigs the game for your enjoyment
				random_list = {
					99 = { change_martial = -1 clr_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
					1 = { clr_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -5 } }
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -3 } }
			}
		}
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.216 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 40
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = wroth } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = cruel } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { is_strong_trigger = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = tall } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = tough } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = bright } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = smart } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = brilliant } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = genius } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = quick } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = dim }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = slow }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = idiot }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = imbecile }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = shy }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = kind }
		}
	}
	option = { # Held up
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh210
		set_character_flag = refused_trauma_start
		prestige = -25
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 5 } }
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 3 } }
			}
		}
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.216 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 60
			modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = craven } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = patient } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = kind } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = slothful } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 FROM = { trait = lunatic_3 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = lunatic_2 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = lunatic_1 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = arbitrary } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = bright }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = quick }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = brilliant }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = genius }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 is_strong_trigger = yes }
		}
	}
}

# slaanesh.211 After the flattery
# The cultist names the flaw: too plain, too thin or too fat.
# Scope: ROOT is the cultist, FROM the target.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.211
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh211
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = refused_flattery_start } }
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh211b trigger = { has_character_flag = refused_flattery_start } }
	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_tempt

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Too plain
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh211
		FROM = {
			clr_character_flag = refused_flattery_start
			character_event = { id = slaanesh.220 days = 5 random = 25 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 34
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				FROM = {
					trait = disfigured
					OR = {
						trait = mut_horns
						trait = mut_scales
						trait = mut_crests
						trait = mut_tongue
						trait = mut_thirdeye
						trait = mut_spines
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = scarred }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = disfigured }
			modifier = { factor = 2.5 trait = burnt_by_asuryan }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = inbred }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = unsightly }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = beautiful }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = proud }
		}
	}
	option = { # Too thin
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh211
		FROM = {
			clr_character_flag = refused_flattery_start
			character_event = { id = slaanesh.240 days = 5 random = 25 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 33
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 FROM = { trait = is_fat } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = gluttonous }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = is_fat }
		}
	}
	option = { # Too fat
		name = EVTOPTCslaanesh211
		FROM = {
			clr_character_flag = refused_flattery_start
			character_event = { id = slaanesh.260 days = 5 random = 25 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 33
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 FROM = { trait = is_malnourished } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = temperate }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = is_malnourished }
		}
	}
}

# slaanesh.220 Too plain
# The target hears they are too plain and agrees or (with integrity 20 or
# more) refuses. Each round below is the same shape for every flaw.
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROM the cultist.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.220
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh220
		trigger = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh220b
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 10 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh220c
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 10 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 15 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh220d
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 15 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 20 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh220e
