# slaanesh.224 Too plain: reprimanded
# As slaanesh.222, by reprimand.
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROM the cultist.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.224
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh224
		trigger = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh224b
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 10 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh224c
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 10 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 15 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh224d
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 15 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 20 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh224e
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 20 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 25 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh224f
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 25 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh224g
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh224h
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
	}

	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_dungeon

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Convinced
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh224
		trigger = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } } }
		add_trait = proud
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.226 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { # Agree
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh224
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		random_list = {
			50 = { change_learning = 1 set_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
			50 = { }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = fake_skill_s } #Slaanesh rigs the game for your enjoyment
				random_list = {
					99 = { change_learning = -1 clr_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
					1 = { clr_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -5 } add_trait = proud }
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -3 } }
			}
		}
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.221 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 20 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 25 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 35 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = wroth } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = cruel } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = bright } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = smart } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = brilliant } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = genius } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = quick } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = dim }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = slow }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = idiot }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = imbecile }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = shy }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = trusting }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 OR = { trait = plain trait = ugly trait = unsightly } }
		}
	}
	option = { # Refuse
		name = EVTOPTCslaanesh224
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		prestige = -50
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 5 } }
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 3 } }
			}
		}
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.221 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 NOT = { prestige = 200 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = kind } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = dim } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = dumb } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = idiot } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = imbecile } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = slow } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = smart }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = bright }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = brilliant }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = genius }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = paranoid }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 OR = { trait = beautiful trait = fair trait = pretty } }
		}
	}
	option = { # End the quirking
		name = EVTOPTDslaanesh224
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
		prestige = -175
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.225 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 50 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 60 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = kind } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = dim } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = dumb } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = idiot } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = imbecile } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = slow } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = smart }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = bright }

			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = genius }

# slaanesh.225 Too plain: the target holds out
# The target ends the quirk. Integrity resets and the cultist leaves them
# alone for a year.
# Scope: ROOT is the cultist, FROM the target.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.225
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh225 trigger = { FROM = { NOT = { is_female = yes } } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh225b trigger = { FROM = { is_female = yes } } }
	picture = GFX_evt_recovery

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh225
		remove_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_quirking }
		FROM = {
			set_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 0 }
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_recently_quirked years = 1 }
		}

		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# slaanesh.226 Too plain: the change
# The target tries to fix their looks: scarred, disfigured, or (with magic)
# more beautiful. Integrity resets and the quirk ends.
# Scope: ROOT is the cultist, FROM the target.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.226
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				FROM = { add_trait = scarred character_event = { id = slaanesh.227 days = 1 } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { trait = scarred } }
			}
			30 = {
				FROM = { add_trait = disfigured character_event = { id = slaanesh.229 days = 1 } }
			}
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { trait = beautiful } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					FROM = {
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_1 }
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_2 }
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_3 }
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_4 }
					}
				}
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 FROM = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_1 } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_2 } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 FROM = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_3 } }
				modifier = { factor = 3 FROM = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_4 } }
				FROM = {
					character_event = { id = slaanesh.231 days = 1 }

					if = { limit = { trait = pretty } remove_trait = pretty add_trait = beautiful }
					if = { limit = { trait = fair } remove_trait = fair add_trait = pretty }
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { trait = beautiful }
							NOT = { trait = pretty }
							NOT = { trait = fair }
							NOT = { trait = plain }
							NOT = { trait = ugly }
							NOT = { trait = unsightly }
						}
						add_trait = fair
					}
					if = { limit = { trait = plain } remove_trait = plain }
					if = { limit = { trait = ugly } remove_trait = ugly add_trait = plain }
					if = { limit = { trait = unsightly } remove_trait = unsightly add_trait = ugly }
				}
			}
		}
		remove_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_quirking }
		FROM = {
			set_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 0 }
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_recently_quirked years = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# slaanesh.240 Too thin

	}

		trigger = {

			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 10 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh240c
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 10 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 15 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh240d
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 15 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 20 } }
		}
	}

		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 20 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 25 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh240f
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 25 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh240g
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh240h
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
	}

	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_tempt

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Agree
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh240
		random_list = {
			50 = { change_diplomacy = 1 set_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
			50 = { }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = fake_skill_s } #Slaanesh rigs the game for your enjoyment
				random_list = {
					99 = { change_diplomacy = -1 clr_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
					1 = { clr_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -5 } }
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -3 } }
			}
		}
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.241 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = gluttonous }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = greedy }

			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = hedonist }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = is_malnourished }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = gregarious }

		}
	}
	option = { # Refuse
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh240
		prestige = -50

			random_list = {
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 5 } }

			}

			factor = 80

		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.244 days = 10 random = 30 } } #reprimand (works better for virtuous)

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 33
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = honest }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = wroth }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = cruel }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = slothful }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = arbitrary }
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				FROM = { OR = { trait = quick trait = smart trait = brilliant trait = genius } }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = craven } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = temperate } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = shy } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = humble } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = is_fat } }
		}

	}

		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { # Agree
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh242
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		random_list = {
			50 = { change_diplomacy = 1 set_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
			50 = { }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -5 }
					add_trait = gluttonous
				}
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -3 } }
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = fake_skill_s } #Slaanesh rigs the game for your enjoyment
				random_list = {
					99 = { change_diplomacy = -1 clr_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
					1 = { clr_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.241 days = 1 } }

			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 20 } }

			modifier = { factor = 0.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 35 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }

			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = slow } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = smart }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = bright }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = brilliant }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = genius }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = paranoid }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = is_fat }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = temperate }
		}
	}
}

# slaanesh.246 Too thin: the change
# The target eats: no longer underfed, now fat, or (if already fat) unable to
# get out of bed.
# Scope: ROOT is the cultist, FROM the target.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.246
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		remove_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_quirking }
		FROM = {
			set_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 0 }
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_recently_quirked years = 1 }
		}
		FROM = {
			if = {
				limit = { trait = is_fat }
				character_event = { id = slaanesh.251 days = 20 random = 30 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { trait = is_fat } NOT = { trait = is_malnourished } }
				character_event = { id = slaanesh.249 days = 20 random = 30 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { trait = is_malnourished }
				remove_trait = is_malnourished
				character_event = { id = slaanesh.247 days = 20 random = 30 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaanesh.260 Too fat
# The target hears they are too fat. Rounds as in slaanesh.220-226.
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROM the cultist.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.260
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh260
		trigger = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh260b
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 10 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh260c
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 10 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 15 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh260d
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 15 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 20 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh260e
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 20 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 25 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh260f
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 25 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh260g
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh260h
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
	}

	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_cult_2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Agree
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh260
		random_list = {
			50 = { change_diplomacy = 1 set_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
			50 = { }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = fake_skill_s } #Slaanesh rigs the game for your enjoyment
				random_list = {
					99 = { change_diplomacy = -1 clr_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
					1 = { clr_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -5 } }
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -3 } }
			}
		}
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.261 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = diligent }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = greedy }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = humble }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = temperate }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = is_fat }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = shy }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = deceitful } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = slaanesh_demon } }
		}
	}
	option = { # Refuse
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh260
		prestige = -50
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 5 } }
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 3 } }
			}
		}
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.261 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 80
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = gluttonous }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = hedonist }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = is_malnourished }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = honest } }
		}
	}
}

		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 10 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh262c
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 10 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 15 } }
		}
	}

		text = EVTDESCslaanesh262d
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 15 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 20 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {

	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Convinced
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh262
		add_trait = temperate
		trigger = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } } }
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.266 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { # Agree
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh262
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		random_list = {
			50 = { change_diplomacy = 1 set_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
			50 = { }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -5 }
					add_trait = temperate
				}
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -3 } }
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = fake_skill_s } #Slaanesh rigs the game for your enjoyment
				random_list = {
					99 = { change_diplomacy = -1 clr_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
					1 = { clr_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.261 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 20 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 25 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 35 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = deceitful } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = bright } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = smart } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = brilliant } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = genius } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = quick } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = dim }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = slow }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = idiot }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = imbecile }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = temperate }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = shy }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = diligent }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = is_fat }
		}
	}
	option = { # Refuse
		name = EVTOPTCslaanesh262
		prestige = -50
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 5 } }
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 3 } }
			}
		}
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.261 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 NOT = { prestige = 200 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = honest } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = dim } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = dumb } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = imbecile } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = idiot } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = slow } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = gluttonous }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = quick }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = smart }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = brilliant }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = genius }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = content }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = is_malnourished }
		}
	}
	option = { # End the quirking
		name = EVTOPTDslaanesh262
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
		prestige = -175
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.265 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = honest } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = dim } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = dumb } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = imbecile } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = idiot } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = slow } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = gluttonous }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = quick }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = smart }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = brilliant }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = genius }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = content }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = is_malnourished }

			modifier = { factor = 1.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 } }

# slaanesh.265 Too fat: the target holds out
# As slaanesh.225.
# Scope: ROOT is the cultist, FROM the target.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.265
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh265 trigger = { FROM = { NOT = { is_female = yes } } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh265b trigger = { FROM = { is_female = yes } } }
	picture = GFX_evt_recovery

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh265
		remove_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_quirking }
		FROM = {
			set_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 0 }
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_recently_quirked years = 1 }
		}

		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# slaanesh.266 Too fat: the change
# The target starves: no longer fat, now underfed, or (if already thin)
# unable to get out of bed.
# Scope: ROOT is the cultist, FROM the target.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.266
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		remove_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_quirking }
		FROM = {
			set_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 0 }
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_recently_quirked years = 1 }
		}
		FROM = {
			if = {
				limit = { trait = is_malnourished }
				character_event = { id = slaanesh.271 days = 20 random = 30 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { trait = is_fat } NOT = { trait = is_malnourished } }
				character_event = { id = slaanesh.269 days = 20 random = 30 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { trait = is_fat }
				character_event = { id = slaanesh.267 days = 20 random = 30 }
			}
		}
	}
}

		}

			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 15 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 20 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh284e
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 20 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 25 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh284f
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 25 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh284g
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh284h
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
	}

	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_dungeon

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Convinced
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh284
		trigger = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } } }
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.286 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { # Agree
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh284
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		random_list = {
			50 = { change_diplomacy = 1 set_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
			50 = { }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = fake_skill_s } #Slaanesh rigs the game for your enjoyment
				random_list = {
					99 = { change_diplomacy = -1 clr_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
					1 = { clr_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -5 } }
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -3 } }
			}
		}
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.281 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 20 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 25 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 35 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = wroth } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = cruel } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { is_strong_trigger = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = tough } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = bright } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = smart } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = brilliant } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = genius } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = quick } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = dim }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = slow }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = idiot }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = imbecile }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = craven }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = shy }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = lustful }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = weak }
		}
	}
	option = { # Refuse
		name = EVTOPTCslaanesh284
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		prestige = -50
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 5 } }
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 3 } }
			}
		}
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.281 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 NOT = { prestige = 200 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = kind } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = dim } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = dumb } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = idiot } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = imbecile } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = slow } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = weak } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = smart }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = bright }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = brilliant }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = genius }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = paranoid }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 is_strong_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = chaste }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = celibate }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = tough }
		}
	}
	option = { # End the quirking
		name = EVTOPTDslaanesh284
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
		prestige = -175
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.285 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 50 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 60 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = kind } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = dim } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = dumb } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = idiot } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = imbecile } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = slow } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = weak } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = smart }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = bright }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = brilliant }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = genius }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = paranoid }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = chaste }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = celibate }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 is_strong_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = tough }
		}
	}
}

# slaanesh.285 Too chaste: the target holds out
# As slaanesh.225.
# Scope: ROOT is the cultist, FROM the target.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.285
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh285 trigger = { FROM = { NOT = { is_female = yes } } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh285b trigger = { FROM = { is_female = yes } } }
	picture = GFX_evt_recovery

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh285
		remove_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_quirking }
		FROM = {
			set_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 0 }
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_recently_quirked years = 1 }
		}

		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# slaanesh.286 Too chaste: the change
# The target turns lustful, cruel, or (if already lustful) depressed.
# Scope: ROOT is the cultist, FROM the target.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.286
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		remove_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_quirking }
		FROM = {
			set_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 0 }
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_recently_quirked years = 1 }
		}
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				FROM = {
					remove_trait = celibate
					remove_trait = chaste
					add_trait = lustful
					character_event = { id = slaanesh.287 days = 1 }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 FROM = { trait = lustful } }
			}
			30 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { trait = cruel } }
				FROM = {
					remove_trait = kind
					add_trait = cruel
					character_event = { id = slaanesh.289 days = 1 }
				}
			}
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 FROM = { NOT = { trait = lustful } } }
				FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.291 days = 1 } add_trait = depressed }
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaanesh.300 Too modest
# The target hears they are too modest. Rounds as in slaanesh.280-286.
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROM the cultist.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.300
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh300
		trigger = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh300b
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 10 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh300c
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 10 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 15 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh300d
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 15 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 20 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh300e
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 20 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 25 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh300f
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 25 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh300g
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh300h
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
	}

	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_cultist

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Agree
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh300
		random_list = {
			50 = { change_diplomacy = 1 set_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
			50 = { }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = fake_skill_s } #Slaanesh rigs the game for your enjoyment
				random_list = {
					99 = { change_diplomacy = -1 clr_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
					1 = { clr_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -5 } }
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -3 } }
			}
		}
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.301 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = greedy }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = diligent }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = envious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = ambitious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = deceitful } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = slaanesh_demon } }
		}
	}
	option = { # Refuse
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh300
		prestige = -50
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 5 } }
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 3 } }
			}
		}
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.301 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 80
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = slothful }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = humble }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = content }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = honest } }
		}
	}
}

	}

			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_recently_quirked years = 1 }
		}
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				FROM = {
					remove_trait = humble
					add_trait = proud
					character_event = { id = slaanesh.307 days = 1 }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 FROM = { trait = proud } }
			}
			30 = {
				FROM = {
					remove_trait = content
					add_trait = ambitious
					character_event = { id = slaanesh.309 days = 1 }
				}
			}
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 FROM = { trait = proud } }
				FROM = {
					character_event = { id = slaanesh.311 days = 1 }
					remove_trait = kind
					add_trait = envious
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# slaanesh.320 Too serious
# The target hears they are too serious. Rounds as in slaanesh.280-286.
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROM the cultist.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.320
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh320
		trigger = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh320b
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 10 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh320c
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 10 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 15 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh320d
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 15 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 20 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh320e
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 20 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 25 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh320f
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 25 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh320g
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh320h
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
	}

	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_cultist

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Agree
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh320
		random_list = {
			50 = { change_diplomacy = 1 set_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
			50 = { }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = fake_skill_s } #Slaanesh rigs the game for your enjoyment
				random_list = {
					99 = { change_diplomacy = -1 clr_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
					1 = { clr_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -5 } }
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -3 } }
			}
		}
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.321 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = hedonist }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = greedy }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = slothful }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = drunkard }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = addict }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = deceitful } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = slaanesh_demon } }
		}
	}
	option = { # Refuse
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh320
		prestige = -50
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 5 } }
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 3 } }
			}
		}
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.321 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 80
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = diligent }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = humble }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = ambitious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = charitable }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = honest } }
		}
	}
}

			NOT = { FROM = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 20 } } }
		}

			NOT = { FROM = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 } } }

			NOT = { FROM = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh321h
		trigger = { FROM = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } } }
	}

	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_cult_1

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh321

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 34
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = hedonist }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = bright }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = smart }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = brilliant }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = genius }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = quick }
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				FROM = { OR = { trait = bright trait = smart trait = brilliant trait = genius } }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = proud } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = slothful } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = hedonist } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = lustful } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = drunkard } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = addict } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh321
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.323 days = 10 random = 30 } } #Trick (works better for virtuous and stupid)

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 33
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = deceitful }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = paranoid }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = envious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = cruel }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = arbitrary }
			modifier = {

				FROM = { OR = { trait = dim trait = slow trait = idiot trait = imbecile } }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = trusting } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = diligent } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = chaste } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = temperate } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaanesh321
		trigger = { OR = { trait = slaanesh_demon is_liege_of = FROM } }

			factor = 33

# slaanesh.322 Too serious: tempted
# As slaanesh.282.
# Scope: ROOT is the target, FROM the cultist.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.322
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh322
		trigger = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh322b
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 10 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh322c
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 10 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 15 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh322d
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 15 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 20 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh322e
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 20 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 25 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh322f
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 25 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh322g
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh322h
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
	}

	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_feast

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Convinced
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh322
		trigger = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } } }
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.326 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = { # Agree
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh322
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		random_list = {
			50 = { change_diplomacy = 1 set_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
			50 = { }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -5 } }
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = -3 } }
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = fake_skill_s } #Slaanesh rigs the game for your enjoyment
				random_list = {
					99 = { change_diplomacy = -1 clr_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
					1 = { clr_character_flag = fake_skill_s }
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.321 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 20 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 25 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 35 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = gregarious } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = bright } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = smart } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = brilliant } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = genius } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = quick } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = bright }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = smart }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = brilliant }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = genius }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = hedonist }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = lustful }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = gregarious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = drunkard }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = addict }
		}
	}
	option = { # Refuse
		name = EVTOPTCslaanesh322
		prestige = -50
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 5 } }
				50 = { change_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 3 } }
			}
		}
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 1 } }
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.321 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 NOT = { prestige = 200 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = shy } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = dim } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = dumb } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = imbecile } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = idiot } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = slow } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = diligent }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = dim }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = slow }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = idiot }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = imbecile }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = temperate }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = ambitious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = shy }
		}
	}
	option = { # End the quirking
		name = EVTOPTDslaanesh322
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
		prestige = -175
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.325 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = shy } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = dim } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = dumb } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = imbecile } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { trait = idiot } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 FROM = { trait = slow } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = diligent }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = dim }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = slow }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = idiot }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = imbecile }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = temperate }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = ambitious }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = shy }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 30 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 40 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 50 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 check_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 60 } }
		}
	}

		}

		}

	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh325 trigger = { FROM = { NOT = { is_female = yes } } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh325b trigger = { FROM = { is_female = yes } } }
	picture = GFX_evt_slaanesh_cultist

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh325
		remove_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_quirking }
		FROM = {
			set_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 0 }

		}

		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }

# slaanesh.326 Too serious: the change
# The target takes to drink, to pleasure, or (if already a drunkard) to the
# dust.
# Scope: ROOT is the cultist, FROM the target.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.326
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		remove_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_quirking }
		FROM = {
			set_variable = { which = "s_integrity" value = 0 }
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_recently_quirked years = 1 }
		}
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				FROM = {
					remove_trait = temperate
					add_trait = drunkard
					character_event = { id = slaanesh.327 days = 1 }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { trait = drunkard } }
			}
			30 = {
				FROM = {
					remove_trait = temperate
					add_trait = hedonist
					character_event = { id = slaanesh.329 days = 1 }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { trait = hedonist } }
			}
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { NOT = { trait = drunkard } } }
				FROM = {
					character_event = { id = slaanesh.331 days = 1 }
					remove_trait = temperate
					add_trait = addict
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Polymorph
###############################################################################

# slaanesh.400 The polymorph spell
# A Slaaneshi mage chooses what to turn a prisoner into: a monstrous ungor, a
# toad, a pig, or the other sex. The roll (slaanesh_polymorph_roll_effect)
# weighs both mages' power: success, resistance, or the spell turned back.
# Fired by the vassal_polymorph decision.
# Scope: ROOT is the caster, FROM the prisoner.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.400
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh400
	picture = GFX_evt_mage_lore_slaanesh

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { # Make it monstrous
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh400
		trigger = { FROM = { NOR = { trait = monstrous trait = creature_beastman } } }
		set_character_flag = slaanesh_polymorph_monster
		hidden_effect = { slaanesh_polymorph_roll_effect = yes }
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
	}
	option = { # Make it a toad
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh400
		trigger = { FROM = { NOR = { trait = creature_toad trait = creature_pig } } }
		set_character_flag = slaanesh_polymorph_toad
		hidden_effect = { slaanesh_polymorph_roll_effect = yes }
		ai_chance = { factor = 40 }
	}
	option = { # Make it a pig
		name = EVTOPTCslaanesh400
		trigger = { FROM = { NOR = { trait = creature_toad trait = creature_pig } } }
		set_character_flag = slaanesh_polymorph_pig
		hidden_effect = { slaanesh_polymorph_roll_effect = yes }
		ai_chance = { factor = 40 }
	}
	option = { # Change its sex
		name = EVTOPTDslaanesh400
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				is_adult = yes
				NOT = { trait = transgender }
				OR = {
					trait = creature_human
					trait = creature_halfling
					trait = creature_dwarf
					trait = creature_chaos_dwarf
					trait = creature_amazon
					trait = creature_elf
				}
			}
		}
		set_character_flag = slaanesh_polymorph_swap
		hidden_effect = { slaanesh_polymorph_roll_effect = yes }
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
	}
}

# slaanesh.401 Monstrous
# The prisoner becomes a monstrous ungor and is set loose. Family and the
# caster's Asur vassals are outraged.
# Scope: ROOT is the prisoner, FROM the caster.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.401
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh401
	picture = GFX_evt_mage_lore_slaanesh

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh401
		set_graphical_culture = beastman_ungor
		add_trait = monstrous
		prisoner = no
		opinion = { modifier = opinion_polymorphed_me who = FROM }
		slaanesh_polymorph_outrage_effect = yes
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.402 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# slaanesh.402 Monstrous (caster)
# The caster's court is scared into line.
# Scope: ROOT is the caster, FROM the prisoner.
character_event = {

# slaanesh.407 A toad
# The prisoner becomes a toad, loses their inheritance and is set loose.
# Scope: ROOT is the prisoner, FROM the caster.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.407
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh407
	picture = GFX_evt_mage_lore_slaanesh

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh407
		set_graphical_culture = averlander
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_trait = creature_elf
			remove_trait = creature_chaos_dwarf
			remove_trait = creature_dwarf
			remove_trait = creature_human
			remove_trait = creature_amazon
			remove_trait = creature_beastman
			remove_trait = resting
			remove_trait = avatar_isha
			remove_trait = avatar_kurnous
		}
		add_trait = creature_toad
		add_trait = disinherited
		prisoner = no
		opinion = { modifier = opinion_polymorphed_master who = FROM }
		slaanesh_polymorph_outrage_effect = yes
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.408 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# slaanesh.417 The other sex
# The prisoner's sex is changed (slaanesh_gender_swap_effect) and they may
# become infertile. Family and the caster's Asur vassals are outraged.
# Scope: ROOT is the prisoner, FROM the caster.
long_character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.417
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh417
		trigger = { is_female = no OR = { trait = creature_human trait = creature_halfling } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh417b
		trigger = { is_female = yes OR = { trait = creature_human trait = creature_halfling } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh417c
		trigger = { is_female = no OR = { trait = creature_dwarf trait = creature_chaos_dwarf } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh417d
		trigger = { is_female = yes OR = { trait = creature_dwarf trait = creature_chaos_dwarf } }
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh417e trigger = { is_female = no trait = creature_elf } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh417f trigger = { is_female = yes trait = creature_elf } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh417g trigger = { is_female = no trait = creature_beastman } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh417h trigger = { is_female = yes trait = creature_beastman } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh417i trigger = { is_female = no trait = creature_amazon } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh417j trigger = { is_female = yes trait = creature_amazon } }
	picture = GFX_evt_mage_lore_slaanesh

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh417
		random = { chance = 50 add_trait = infertile }
		slaanesh_gender_swap_effect = yes
		opinion = { modifier = opinion_polymorphed_me who = FROM }
		slaanesh_polymorph_outrage_effect = yes
		FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.418 } }
	}
}

# slaanesh.425 The monstrous child
# A child of a monstrous parent, born with ungor looks, is monstrous too.
# Fired from on_post_birth.
# Scope: ROOT is the child.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.425
	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		graphical_culture = ungorgfx
		OR = {
			mother_even_if_dead = { trait = monstrous }
			father_even_if_dead = { trait = monstrous }
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		add_trait = monstrous
	}
}

# slaanesh.450 The Dark Prince's caprice
# Slaanesh changes the cultist's sex (slaanesh_gender_swap_effect). A
# hidden cultist's secret spreads.
# Scope: ROOT is the cultist.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.450
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh450
		trigger = { is_female = no OR = { trait = creature_human trait = creature_halfling } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh450b
		trigger = { is_female = yes OR = { trait = creature_human trait = creature_halfling } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh450c
		trigger = { is_female = no OR = { trait = creature_dwarf trait = creature_chaos_dwarf } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCslaanesh450d
		trigger = { is_female = yes OR = { trait = creature_dwarf trait = creature_chaos_dwarf } }
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh450e trigger = { is_female = no trait = creature_elf } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh450f trigger = { is_female = yes trait = creature_elf } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh450g trigger = { is_female = no trait = creature_beastman } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh450h trigger = { is_female = yes trait = creature_beastman } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh450i trigger = { is_female = no trait = creature_amazon } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh450j trigger = { is_female = yes trait = creature_amazon } }
	picture = GFX_evt_mage_lore_slaanesh

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh450
		slaanesh_gender_swap_effect = yes
		if = { limit = { slaanesh_open_worshipper_trigger = no } increase_visibility_effect = yes }
	}
}

# slaanesh.451 Deposed for their sex
# A ruler whose realm's succession law no longer fits their new sex
# abdicates.
# Scope: ROOT is the changed character.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.451
	desc = EVTDESCslaanesh451
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh451 trigger = { NOT = { is_female = yes } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh451b trigger = { is_female = yes } }

	picture = GFX_evt_bad_news

	trigger = {
		is_ruler = yes
		OR = {
			AND = {
				is_female = yes #the game still considers her a female
				primary_title = { has_law = enatic_succession }
			}
			AND = {
				is_female = no #the game still considers him a female
				primary_title = { has_law = agnatic_succession }
			}
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh451
		abdicate = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# slaanesh.452 Settling in (once same-sex attracted)
# The changed character's desires settle; their spouse hears of it
# (slaanesh.454).
# Scope: ROOT is the changed character.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.452
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh452 trigger = { NOT = { is_female = yes } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh452b trigger = { is_female = yes } }
	picture = GFX_evt_mage_lore_slaanesh

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh452
		remove_trait = homosexual
		hidden_effect = {
			spouse = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.454 days = 150 random = 100 } }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# slaanesh.454 The spouse's choice
# The spouse accepts the change (slaanesh.455) or ends the marriage
# (slaanesh.456).
# Scope: ROOT is the spouse, FROM the changed character.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.454
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh454 trigger = { NOT = { is_female = yes } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh454b trigger = { is_female = yes } }
	picture = GFX_evt_mage_lore_slaanesh

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh454
		trigger = { OR = { trait = homosexual ai = no } } #A wish come true
		hidden_effect = { spouse = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.455 } } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBslaanesh454
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = homosexual } } #This marriage is over
		hidden_effect = { spouse = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.456 } } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCslaanesh454
		trigger = { ai = no } #I don't care
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# slaanesh.455 The spouse stays

		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh458
		add_lover = FROM
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# slaanesh.459 The spouse leaves
# As slaanesh.456.
# Scope: ROOT is the changed character, FROM the spouse.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.459
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh459 trigger = { NOT = { is_female = yes } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCslaanesh459b trigger = { is_female = yes } }
	picture = GFX_evt_mage_lore_slaanesh

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAslaanesh459
		remove_spouse = FROM
		prestige = -100
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# slaanesh.460 A child after all
# A changed woman whose wife is fertile can still conceive with her
# (consort.0009). Only characters with the slaanesh_gender_swapped flag are
# checked.
# Scope: ROOT is the changed character.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.460

	has_character_flag = slaanesh_gender_swapped
	min_age = 15
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	only_women = yes

	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = transgender
		NOT = { trait = creature_orc } #Infertile species
		NOT = { trait = creature_goblin }
		NOT = { trait = creature_hobgoblin }
		NOT = { trait = creature_snotling }
		NOT = { trait = creature_nehekharan }
		is_vampire_trigger = no
		NOT = { trait = celibate }
		NOT = { trait = infertile }
		OR = {
			NOT = { age = 45 }
			AND = { NOT = { trait = creature_human } NOT = { trait = creature_halfling } }
		}
		is_pregnant = no
		spouse = {
			is_female = yes
			NOT = { trait = eunuch }
			NOT = { trait = creature_orc } #Infertile species
			NOT = { trait = creature_goblin }
			NOT = { trait = creature_hobgoblin }
			NOT = { trait = creature_snotling }
			NOT = { trait = creature_nehekharan }
			is_vampire_trigger = no

			#To avoid halfbreeds
			OR = {
				AND = { ROOT = { trait = creature_human } trait = creature_human }
				AND = { ROOT = { trait = creature_amazon } trait = creature_amazon }
				AND = { ROOT = { trait = creature_elf } trait = creature_elf }
				AND = { ROOT = { trait = creature_halfling } trait = creature_halfling }
				AND = { ROOT = { trait = creature_dwarf } trait = creature_dwarf }
				AND = { ROOT = { trait = creature_chaos_dwarf } trait = creature_chaos_dwarf }
				AND = { ROOT = { trait = creature_skaven } trait = creature_skaven }
			}

			is_alive = yes
			NOT = { trait = incapable }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = stage_progressed } #so that the consorts will cycle
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		years = 15
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 war = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = creature_elf }
		modifier = { factor = 45 in_command = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.45 trait = retired_with_harem }
		modifier = { factor = 0.15 has_character_modifier = horny_harem_mod }
		modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = lustful }
		modifier = { factor = 4 trait = shy }
		modifier = { factor = 0.9 trait = gregarious }
		modifier = { factor = 0.85 trait = hedonist }
		modifier = { factor = 4 trait = chaste }
		modifier = { factor = 4 trait = ill }
		modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = stressed }
		modifier = { factor = 2.75 trait = depressed }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = kind }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = just }
		modifier = { factor = 0.95 trait = cruel }
		modifier = { factor = 0.95 trait = arbitrary }
		modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = possessed }
		modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = hunchback }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = clubfooted }
		modifier = { factor = 4 trait = pneumonic }
		modifier = { factor = 3.75 trait = homosexual }
		modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = dwarf }
		modifier = { factor = 2.75 trait = inbred }
		modifier = { factor = 0.95 is_strong_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = weak }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = scholar }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = gardener }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = hunter }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = duelist }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = falconer }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = mystic }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = temperate }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = charitable }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = slothful }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = diligent }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = patient }
		modifier = { factor = 1.15 trait = humble }
		modifier = { factor = 1.1 trait = craven }
		modifier = { factor = 0.9 trait = brave }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = paranoid }
		modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = trusting }
		modifier = { factor = 100 trait = incapable }
		modifier = { factor = 10 trait = infirm }
		modifier = { factor = 15 trait = leper }
		modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = disfigured }
		modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = wounded }
		modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = maimed }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = blinded }
		modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = is_fat }
		modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = is_malnourished }
		modifier = { factor = 0.9 trait = drunkard }
		modifier = { factor = 1.2 trait = addict }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = diligent }
		modifier = { factor = 100 trait = creature_human NOT = { fertility = 0.01 } }
		modifier = { factor = 4 trait = creature_human fertility = 0 NOT = { fertility = 0.1 } }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = creature_human fertility = 0.1 NOT = { fertility = 0.3 } }
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			trait = creature_human
			fertility = 0.3
			NOT = { fertility = 0.5 }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.9
			trait = creature_human
			fertility = 0.5
			NOT = { fertility = 0.75 }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.85
			trait = creature_human
			fertility = 0.75
			NOT = { fertility = 0.9 }
		}
		modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = creature_human fertility = 0.9 NOT = { fertility = 1.0 } }
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.75
			trait = creature_human
			fertility = 1.0
			NOT = { fertility = 1.1 }
		}
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = creature_human fertility = 1.1 NOT = { fertility = 1.2 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.6 trait = creature_human fertility = 1.2 NOT = { fertility = 1.4 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = creature_human fertility = 1.4 NOT = { fertility = 1.6 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = creature_human fertility = 1.6 }
		modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = creature_human NOT = { age = 18 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.65 trait = creature_human age = 18 NOT = { age = 30 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.85 trait = creature_human age = 30 NOT = { age = 40 } }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = creature_human age = 40 NOT = { age = 55 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.95 num_of_children = 0 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.85 num_of_children = 1 NOT = { num_of_children = 5 } }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 num_of_children = 5 NOT = { num_of_children = 10 } }
		modifier = { factor = 2.5 num_of_children = 10 }
		modifier = { factor = 0 num_of_children = 5 trait = creature_elf }
		modifier = { factor = 10 trait = creature_human age = 55 }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = misguided_warrior }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = tough_soldier }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = skilled_tactician }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = brilliant_strategist }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = naive_appeaser }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = underhanded_rogue }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = charismatic_negotiator }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = grey_eminence }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = misguided_warrior }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = tough_soldier }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = skilled_tactician }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = brilliant_strategist }
	}

	immediate = {
		spouse = {
			character_event = { id = slaanesh.461 } #pingy ping
		}
	}
}

# slaanesh.461 Tell the spouse
# Passes the pregnancy on (consort.0009).
# Scope: ROOT is the spouse, FROM the changed character.
character_event = {
	id = slaanesh.461
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { character_event = { id = consort.0009 } }
	}
}
